# Skills · S · part 223

824 entries, survey-review to svelte. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **survey-review**: Use when running auto-improvement rounds on a written AutoSurvey draft, or when /survey-review is invoked. each round runs **2 reviewer personas** (Senior Reviewer + Skeptic) in parallel fresh threads; the author addresses every demand with accept/partial/reject + reason. The demands are presented as a human checkpoint between rounds (--auto-confirm skips it for CI). 2 rounds by default. ([BIGBALLON/AutoSurvey](https://github.com/BIGBALLON/AutoSurvey/tree/HEAD/skills/survey-review) · ★2)
- **survey-review**: Reads a land survey the way a design team has to: establishes what kind of survey it is and therefore what it is certified to show, checks it against what the project actually needs, and reports the gaps before a scheme gets built on top of them. Separates what was measured from what was compiled from records, works the boundary, the encumbrances, the topography, the utilities and the datum in that order, and flags every place the drawing shows a line that no instrument supports. Trigger on "review the survey", "is this survey adequate", "what's missing from this survey", "ALTA survey review"… ([DavidCho1999/archaiflow-skills](https://github.com/DavidCho1999/archaiflow-skills/tree/HEAD/bundles/code-life-safety/skills/survey-review) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **survey-run**: Use when generating a full survey paper from a structured brief, or when /survey-run is invoked. End-to-end AutoSurvey orchestrator — runs the resumable phase-driven pipeline (Phase 1 Drafting: init → refine_brief → search → thesis → outline-sketch; Phase 2 Arguing: per-section claim-mining + 5-step skeleton; Phase 3 Polishing: 2-persona review → audits → compile + evidence dashboard) tracked in state.json. ([BIGBALLON/AutoSurvey](https://github.com/BIGBALLON/AutoSurvey/tree/HEAD/skills/survey-run) · ★2)

- **survey-to-deck**: 端到端"调研一个方向 → 深读论文 → 每篇产两份报告 → 入 OmniBox 向量语义索引 → 做成 Slidev 串讲 deck"的总编排(meta)skill。 它本身不重新实现任何一步,而是把四个已有 skill 串成一条流水线并用 Workflow 并行 fan-out: ① 发现(comprehensive-survey / 用户直接给清单)→ ② 去重 → ③ 每篇一个 agent 跑 arxiv-deepdive 写 01_highlevel + 02_technical(带 frontmatter)→ ④ update_index.py 增量入库 + search.py 验证 → ⑤ paper-talk-deck 出 deck。 Use PROACTIVELY whenever the user wants the WHOLE pipeline from a topic/paper-list to a knowledge-base + presentation — triggers: "调研 &lt;方向&gt; 然后读论文出报告入库再做 deck", "把这批 paper 跑全套:deep-read 进 omnibox + re-index + 出一个 deck", "深入读一两百篇 paper、每篇 high-level + technical 报告、入向量库、做 s… ([Gonglitian/agent-skills](https://github.com/Gonglitian/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/survey-to-deck) · ★2)

- **sv-callset-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating or reproducing SV caller performance metrics (precision/recall/F1) on simulated or GIAB/Wagner-style truth sets using synthetic long reads, hybrid SLR+long-read data, or low-coverage (e.g. 5x) long-read benchmarks. - **sv-callset-f1-comparison-across-callers**: Use when comparing germline SV calling accuracy of multiple long-read callers on HG002 HiFi WGS data against the GIAB draft T2T SV benchmark (GRCh38_HG2-T2TQ100-V1.1_stvar) or the GIAB CMRG SV benchmark, to obtain size-stratified F1-scores and caller rankings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sv-callset-f1-comparison-across-callers))
- **sv-callset-intersection-with-truth-panels**: Use when a filtered SV VCF or BED callset from long-read (MinION/nanopore) calling must be quantified for recall and precision against an established reference SV panel on a defined region such as NA12878 chromosome 1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sv-callset-intersection-with-truth-panels))
- **sv-cnv-calling**: Use when detecting, merging, filtering, or benchmarking structural variants or copy-number changes from short-read, exome, or long-read data. Not for repeats (repeat-expansions) or paralogs (difficult-genes-and-mito). ([oumarkme/research_agents](https://github.com/oumarkme/research_agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/bioinfo-lab/skills/sv-cnv-calling))
- **sv-composition**: 结合 AKDAgent 本地工具进行旋律、和声、编曲结构和乐句设计，或分析已有音乐段落。仅在用户要求作曲编曲时扩展素材，不把人声调教变成重写歌曲。 ([littleclock2/AKDAgent-Plugin](https://github.com/littleclock2/AKDAgent-Plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/akdagent-chatgpt-plugin/skills/sv-composition))
- **sv-content-quality**: Score a live page's content quality for a target keyword (E-E-A-T and helpful-content criteria) with the SV MCP `content-quality` tool, optionally comparing two URLs. Use after drafting and publishing, or to evaluate an existing page. ([seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills](https://github.com/seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-content-quality) · ★2)
- **sv-content-transformer**: Rewrite or reformat text the user already has into a chosen content type (meta title, product description, landing-page copy and more) with the SV MCP `content-transformer` tool. Use to reshape existing copy. To write new content use sv-seogpt or sv-prose. ([seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills](https://github.com/seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-content-transformer) · ★2)
- **sv-core-analysis**: Run an element-by-element on-page SEO analysis of a URL (status, load speed, title, meta description, headings, keyword usage) and compare it with the top-ranked pages, using the SV MCP `core-analysis` tool. Use to benchmark a page against competitors. ([seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills](https://github.com/seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-core-analysis) · ★2)
- **sv-dev**: Development workflow for Security Verifiers. Use when asked to run tests, lint code, format files, set up the development environment, or perform CI checks on the codebase. ([diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/testing-security/sv-dev) · ★67)
- **sv-diff**: Compare two SystemVerilog/Verilog sources with `verible-verilog-diff` for a semantic, SV-aware diff that ignores whitespace and comment-only changes. Use when the user wants to compare two `.sv` files, or two git revisions of the same file, and plain `diff` would be noisy because of formatting or comments. ([nyavana/fpga-lsp](https://github.com/nyavana/fpga-lsp/tree/HEAD/plugins/fpga-lsp/skills/sv-diff))
- **sv-experiment**: Entry point for experiments whose printed numbers must be traceable: freezing corpus, samples, gold sets and model or prompt versions, the runs log, numbers checked into the paper, and the replication artifact. Routes to one sv-experiment sub-command and says which. Use when setting up, running, reporting or packaging experiments. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-experiment) · ★1)
- **sv-experiment-freeze**: Freezes what an experiment depends on before any model output is seen: the corpus, the sample and gold set, model and prompt versions with a pre-set acceptance bar, and the rule that disagreements become written rules rather than silent fixes. Use when setting up an experiment, building a gold set or annotation round, or changing a prompt or model. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-experiment-freeze) · ★1)
- **sv-experiment-numbers**: Moves numbers from runs into the paper so each one is traceable: a numbers file generated by scripts, macros or tables built from it, a check that every number in the manuscript matches the file, and verified numbers recorded in STATUS before they reach the text. Use when numbers enter or change in the paper, or before submission. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-experiment-numbers) · ★1)
- **sv-experiment-prereg**: Locks a confirmatory analysis before outcomes are seen: hypotheses, the estimand, the exact primary analysis, the decision rule, stopping and multiplicity are written down and frozen as a git commit, and an audit later proves the registration preceded every committed output and was never edited. Anything not registered is reported as exploratory. Use when a hypothesis test, an effect estimate or a p-value will support a claim, before the outcome data is looked at. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-experiment-prereg) · ★1)
- **sv-experiment-replication**: Prepares where data lives and the replication artifact: data placement and compute backend, a manifest of data files that can be rebuilt and verified, an anonymized artifact checked for identity leaks, and the mirror for double-blind review. Use when deciding data storage, packaging a replication artifact, or checking an anonymous mirror. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-experiment-replication) · ★1)
- **sv-experiment-runs**: Keeps the runs log and the state files of a project's experiments: a row per run when it starts with command, code commit, data version, backend, status and outputs, failed runs recorded and never deleted, outputs never overwritten, and diffable state files as the authority over web dashboards. Use when logging runs, reviewing what ran, or deciding where label and queue state lives. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-experiment-runs) · ★1)
- **sv-feature-extraction**: Use when you have a NanoSV VCF of SV calls from long nanopore reads and need per-call numeric features to train or apply a machine-learning filter (e.g. random forest in scikit-learn) that separates true SVs from false positives beyond baseline precision (~54%). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sv-feature-extraction))
- **sv-feature-vector-computation**: Use when you have putative SV calls (VCF/BED from dysgu or gold sets such as GIAB HG001/HG002 or pbsv PacBio calls) and need feature vectors plus quality probabilities for ML-based SV classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sv-feature-vector-computation))
- **sv-feedback**: Entry point for handling feedback: turns comments from advisors, co-authors, reviewers or agents into tracked revisions with IDs, one change per comment and diff verification; records expert guidance with actions and follow-up; and plans peer-review rebuttals by reviewer mindset and strategy. Routes each request to one sv-feedback sub-command and says which. Use when feedback, advice or reviews arrive. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-feedback) · ★1)
- **sv-feedback-guidance**: Records expert guidance on a paper or experiment as a dated guidance record: the scenario shown, verbatim quotes, the interpretation marked as such, points to notice, actions with owners, due dates and done criteria, and the decision; later lists open, overdue and unevidenced actions. Use when advice arrives from a meeting, call or relayed message, or to check which advice was followed up. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-feedback-guidance) · ★1)
- **sv-feedback-rebuttal**: Provides actionable peer-review rebuttal guidance, not finished rebuttal prose: inventories and classifies concerns, matches the reviewer mindset and strategy on ML venues, gives per-concern plans with evidence pointers, and picks a conference, journal or internal response format. Use when the user pastes reviewer comments, asks how to respond to a reviewer, needs a journal revision letter plan, or says the reviewer ignored the rebuttal. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-feedback-rebuttal) · ★1)
- **sv-feedback-revise**: Turns comments on a manuscript into tracked revisions: collects every comment from every channel, gives it an ID, triages it, applies one change per comment with the ID in the commit, keeps note markers until their owner closes them, verifies each claimed change against the actual diff, and fills the response table. Use when inline TeX notes, PDF annotations, chat requests or review reports arrive. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-feedback-revise) · ★1)
- **sv-figure**: Entry point for every figure task: design advice for a paper's core figures, draw.io reconstruction of a reference image, plots from code, TikZ, HTML or SVG figures, posters, and explanatory figures for learning. Routes to one sv-figure sub-command and says which. Use when the user asks to design, draw, plot or rebuild anything visual. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-figure) · ★1)
- **sv-figure-design**: Advises on the design of a technical paper's three core figures: the motivated example (Figure 1), the solution overview, and the experimental results. Identifies the figure type, recommends a paradigm, sketches the layout, guides labels and annotations, suggests a tool, and audits the universal and archive figure rules with severity. Use when the user asks to design, critique or improve a paper figure. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-figure-design) · ★1)

- **sv-figure-plot**: Produces data and mathematical figures from code, saving the figure and the script together: Matplotlib or Seaborn in a reusable plotting script, Plotly for interactive views, vector output sized to the column, and numbers that trace to the project's numbers file. - **sv-figure-poster**: Plans and checks a research poster with the library's figure rules: one message per panel, figures reused from the paper under the universal design rules, a tool chosen from the tool matrix, and the archive figure rules audit. - **sv-figure-tikz**: Builds TikZ and PGFPlots figures for LaTeX papers and decks in a render-and-inspect loop: a standalone render to PDF and PNG, inspection of the PNG for overlaps, clipping and legibility, a short review note, and done only when the inspection is recorded. - **sv-format**: Format a SystemVerilog/Verilog file with `verible-verilog-format`, either in-place (rewrite the file) or in preview mode (print formatted text to stdout, file unchanged). - **sv-post-calling-filtering**: Use when you have a raw NanoSV (or equivalent long-read) SV VCF and need to remove likely false positives, especially calls anchored in homopolymer or tandem-repeat regions and small indels below the 40 bp SV threshold. - **sv-precision-recall-benchmarking**: Use when comparing SV-caller outputs (VCFs from dysgu, manta, delly, lumpy, strelka, gatk) against a reference call set (e.g. NIST_SVs_Integration_v0.6) to quantify per-class precision/recall/F1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sv-precision-recall-benchmarking))
- **sv-preliminary-audit**: Run a quick technical SEO health check on a URL (canonical tags, blocking meta tags, load speed, SSL, robots, sitemap) and get an overall score with the SV MCP `preliminaryaudit` tool. Use as a fast first-pass diagnostic. For an element-by-element review use sv-core-analysis. ([seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills](https://github.com/seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-preliminary-audit) · ★2)
- **sv-project**: 检查、复制、迁移或编辑 SVP 工程文件，包括 SV1/SV2 差异及其他歌声工程转换。保留源文件，区分磁盘编辑与 SV 内实时编辑。 ([littleclock2/AKDAgent-Plugin](https://github.com/littleclock2/AKDAgent-Plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/akdagent-chatgpt-plugin/skills/sv-project))
- **sv-proposal**: Entry point for research proposals: thesis proposals (kaiti reports), grant applications such as NSFC, NSF, ERC and NIH, and research statements or PhD plans, built from a spine, evidence ledger, timeline and risks before drafting. Routes to one sv-proposal sub-command and says which. Use when the user needs a proposal, grant or research statement. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-proposal) · ★1)

Use when the user needs a grant application or a specific aims page. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-proposal-grant) · ★1)
- **sv-proposal-kaiti**: Plans and drafts a thesis proposal (kaiti report): the call sheet from the department's rules, the proposal spine of questions, aims and feasibility, an evidence ledger, section map, timeline and risks, a draft in the Chinese kaiti register with visible gap markers, and a reviewer pass. Use when the user needs a thesis proposal or opening report. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-proposal-kaiti) · ★1)
- **sv-proposal-statement**: Plans and drafts a research statement or a PhD research proposal: the spine of questions, aims and feasibility, an evidence ledger, section map and timeline, a draft in proposal register, and a reviewer pass. Use when the user needs a research statement for the job market or a fellowship, or a research plan for a PhD application. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-proposal-statement) · ★1)
- **sv-prose**: Write a full long-form SEO or GEO article, blog post, guide or landing page with the SV MCP `prose` tool (async). Use when the user wants a complete article on a topic. For short snippets such as meta titles use sv-seogpt. ([seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills](https://github.com/seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-prose) · ★2)
- **sv-quickread**: Entry point for quick reads of one paper: a one-page, five-minute card in nine fixed sections with sourced results, graded contributions and a claim-evidence table, optionally with a Style Card of its writing. Routes to one sv-quickread sub-command and says which. Use when the user hands over one paper and wants its gist quickly. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-quickread) · ★1)
- **sv-quickread-card**: Writes the nine-section quick-read card for one paper: problem, core idea, a field-specific method block, results copied with locations, contributions tagged proved, empirical or conjectured, a claim-evidence table, limitations, and the link to the reader's research. Text through MinerU only; checked and indexed. Use when the user wants one paper summarized quickly or triaged. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-quickread-card) · ★1)
- **sv-quickread-style**: Writes the quick-read card and also dissects the paper's writing into one Paper Style Card (structure, rhetorical moves, sentence templates) in the sv-write-style-mine format, saved beside the card as a candidate for a venue profile. One card never becomes a rule. Use when the user wants a paper read quickly and its writing style analysed for a target venue. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-quickread-style) · ★1)
- **sv-ranklens**: Measure how a website ranks for an entity or keyword across repeated AI-engine queries, and find the competitors that appear, with the SV MCP `ranklens` tool. Use for brand-level AI visibility and competitor benchmarking. ([seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills](https://github.com/seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-ranklens) · ★2)
- **sv-raw-review**: Rules for reading SystemVerilog source directly when the odve-index MCP cannot answer (a tool returned error, the file is in no elaborated unit, the index is unavailable) or when a review genuinely needs the code - how to read as little as possible, by outline and ranges, following filelists not directories, never the UVM library. Use it whenever you are about to Read or cat a .sv/.svh file, grep across odve/, or review generated agent code line by line. ([open-dve/open-dve](https://github.com/open-dve/open-dve/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sv-raw-review) · ★1)
- **sv-recall-fpr-evaluation**: Use when comparing SV caller sensitivity/FPR (e.g., NanoSV vs Lumpy vs Sniffles) from simulated nanopore reads with a known set of rearrangement breakpoints as ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sv-recall-fpr-evaluation))
- **sv-report**: Render the displayable time-step change for a SocioVerse2 study — per-step maps, a metric timeline (with intervention markers), and report.md, all queried from the DuckDB trajectory store. Use after sv-run. ([sii-research/SocioVerse2](https://github.com/sii-research/SocioVerse2/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sv-report) · ★27 · 4 copies)
- **sv-review**: Entry point for reviewing a manuscript before it leaves the group: a read- only pre-submission audit with sub-checklists and a submission-day runbook, or a bounded multi-round check of research fit, venue style and language with a round log. Routes to one sv-review sub-command and says which. Use when the user asks to review, audit, proofread or re-check a draft. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-review) · ★1)
- **sv-review-loop**: Runs a bounded multi-round check over a manuscript: research-writing fit first (claims, scope, hedges, numbers, threats), then venue and field style, then language through a de-AI audit, with a regression re-run of the research check. Applies only authorized edits, guards spans against reversion and locked-decision flips, and writes a dated round log. Use when a draft needs repeated checking before review, submission or camera-ready. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-review-loop) · ★1)
- **sv-review-once**: Runs a read-only pre-submission review of a technical paper across five dimensions (macro logic, writing details, English grammar, LaTeX formatting, figure quality) with CRITICAL, MAJOR and MINOR findings, then the research-core threats, double-blind, artifact, venue-submission and integrity checklists, a devil's-advocate pass, and the submission-day runbook. Use when the user asks to review, audit or proofread a paper before a deadline. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-review-once) · ★1)
- **sv-run**: Execute the longitudinal simulation run for a SocioVerse2 study — bind P + E + decision model + schedule, run the E_t→B_t→E_{t+1} loop, and persist the panel + metrics to DuckDB. Use after the environment and population bundles exist. ([sii-research/SocioVerse2](https://github.com/sii-research/SocioVerse2/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sv-run) · ★27 · 4 copies)
- **sv-score-normalization-across-classifiers**: Use when comparing SV pathogenicity predictions from tools that output scores on different scales, especially when ranking the disease-causative gene for positive-control SVs against a patient-cohort background. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/sv-score-normalization-across-classifiers))
- **sv-seo-mapping**: Map a list of keywords to the most relevant existing pages on a domain, and detect keyword cannibalization, with the SV MCP `seogptmapping` tool (async). Use for site-wide structure, internal linking and content-gap planning. ([seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills](https://github.com/seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-seo-mapping) · ★2)
- **sv-seogpt**: Generate short-form SEO or GEO copy (meta titles, meta descriptions, FAQs, social posts, ad copy) with the SV MCP `seogpt` tool. Use when the user wants one specific short piece of content from a keyword. For full articles use sv-prose. ([seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills](https://github.com/seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-seogpt) · ★2)
- **sv-seogpt-compare**: Get competitive SEO strategy for one URL and one target keyword with the SV MCP `seogptcompare` tool (async), which compares the page against its top competitors. Use when the question is strategic, such as what to prioritize for this page and keyword. ([seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills](https://github.com/seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-seogpt-compare) · ★2)
- **sv-signature-threshold-filtering**: Use when mining a contig-to-reference BAM (primary + supplementary split blocks) for large INS/DEL signatures with the 30 bp Diff_dis / 3000 bp Diff_olp thresholds of the VolcanoSV-vc large indel module. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sv-signature-threshold-filtering))
- **sv-size-and-class-distribution-analysis**: Use when you have long-read (e.g., nanopore NanoSV VCF) SV call sets plus matched short-read caller outputs and need to classify which calls are novel, then profile their size bins, GC content, and retrotransposon (LINE) enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sv-size-and-class-distribution-analysis))
- **sv-slides**: Entry point for LaTeX Beamer decks for math and statistics group meetings: a weekly report that carries last week's next steps forward, or a critical paper talk with estimand, identification and reproducibility slides, compiled, linted and rendered. Routes to one sv-slides sub-command and says which. Use when the user needs group-meeting slides. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-slides) · ★1)
- **sv-slides-talk**: Builds a 12 to 18 slide critical paper talk in Beamer: the paper's section tree mapped to slide groups, plus the critical slides (paper card with estimand and identification, ranked assumptions, results with sources and no causal verbs, claims against evidence, link to the project, reproducibility, a discussion question). Use when the user presents a paper at a group meeting or journal club. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-slides-talk) · ★1)
- **sv-slides-weekly**: Builds the weekly-report Beamer deck: conclusions first, last week's next steps marked done, partial or dropped, results with sources, blockers, decisions needed, and next steps with owner, date and kill criterion; the progress variant groups the same spine by topic. Compiles, lints, renders and exports notes. Use when the user needs this week's group-meeting slides. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-slides-weekly) · ★1)
- **sv-study**: Entry point for understanding-first study: explains a concept, works through a paper or textbook chapter one idea at a time with the learner's restatement and diagnosis, draws explanatory figures, runs optional drills, and bridges learning to the project. Routes to one sv-study sub- command and says which. Use when the user wants to really understand something. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-study) · ★1)
- **sv-study-bridge**: Bridges something just read or learned to the current research project: maps the source setting onto the project, checks which assumptions transfer, gives one of five verdicts on whether and how to use it, and designs the cheapest validation experiment with expected results and a kill criterion fixed in advance. Use when the user asks whether a method just read or studied fits the project. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-study-bridge) · ★1)
- **sv-study-chapter**: Works through a textbook chapter or lecture notes understanding-first: maps the chapter's concepts and their order, checks prerequisites against the learner profile with one short question each, runs the protocol per concept with restatement and diagnosis, and keeps book progress in the study vault. Use when the user wants to study chapter N of a book or a set of lecture notes. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-study-chapter) · ★1)
- **sv-study-drill**: Switches on one of the study drill options that are off by default: Socratic dialogue, Feynman explain-back, light self-test with confidence ratings, proof reconstruction with gaps for the learner, notes only, or Anki export of approved concept notes. High-confidence wrong answers go to the misconceptions file. Use when the user asks to be quizzed, tested, questioned Socratically, or to export cards. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-study-drill) · ★1)
- **sv-study-paper**: Works through one paper understanding-first: official PDF first through MinerU, the concepts the paper needs planned in dependency order, the protocol run per concept with restatement and diagnosis, formulas and numbers copied with locations, and the session saved to the paper folder and the study vault. Use when the user wants a close reading of a paper rather than a quick card. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-study-paper) · ★1)
- **sv-study-quick-explain**: Explains one concept understanding-first with no source material: plain principle, motivation, a minimal example, links to what the learner already knows, one or two pictures, then the learner's restatement, diagnosis against the expert mental model and correction. Uses the study vault's profile when it exists. Use when the user asks to understand or explain a single concept. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-study-quick-explain) · ★1)
- **sv-study-visual-explain**: Draws the motivation or structure figure for the concept being studied, as the study protocol's picture step: picks a kit template, fills it from the session's content without inventing numbers, renders and inspects it, and saves the HTML and PNG next to the notes. A thin entry to the figure group's explain sub-command. Use when a study session needs a picture. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-study-visual-explain) · ★1)
- **sv-supervisor**: Entry point for running a research project: a start-up interview that writes research.yaml and docs, a session brief with stage, deadline, blockers and next skills, project records (STATUS, TODO, decisions, logs), and lessons harvested into research-core. Routes to one sv-supervisor sub- command and says which. Use when starting a session or project, or updating records. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-supervisor) · ★1)
- **sv-supervisor-brief**: Gives a read-only session brief for a research project: the stage in research.yaml and the stage the repository signals suggest, the next deadline with its source, blockers, overdue guidance, open reviewer markers and records warnings, then routes to one to three skills with a reason each. Routes reading and learning requests by intent. Never does the work. Use when starting a session or asking what to do next. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-supervisor-brief) · ★1)
- **sv-supervisor-harvest**: Turns a project's feedback tables, guidance records, decision records, review-loop logs, STATUS and git history, plus expert notes, into sourced and deduplicated rule candidates for research-core, classifies where each belongs, and proposes the change on a learn branch for the owner to approve. Also starts a new field profile. Use when a milestone is reached, after a submission, or when a new field needs a profile. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-supervisor-harvest) · ★1)
- **sv-supervisor-init**: Starts a research project: interviews the user one short block at a time (field, paper type, stage, venue and deadline with its official source, repositories and the one writable source of truth, data and compute, records and co-author note macros), writes research.yaml after showing the diff, and sets up docs with STATUS, TODO, ADR 0001 and logs. Use when a project or repository has no research.yaml yet. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-supervisor-init) · ★1)
- **sv-supervisor-records**: Keeps a research project's records outside the paper: STATUS with the current state only, TODO, numbered decision records, dated logs and group-meeting logs, sources of truth for paper, data and numbers with a sync guard, the notes lifecycle, de-accretion of docs, and an optional Linear mirror. Use when updating status, recording a decision, naming a source of truth, syncing a co-edited paper, or cleaning up project docs. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-supervisor-records) · ★1)
- **sv-top-competitors**: List the top-ranking competitor URLs for a keyword with the SV MCP `top-competitors` tool. Use early in a competitive analysis to decide who to benchmark against. For a full comparison of one URL against its competitors use sv-seogpt-compare. ([seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills](https://github.com/seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-top-competitors) · ★2)
- **sv-topical-authority**: Build a topical content plan for a keyword (suggested article topics, plus related subtopics in SEO mode) with the SV MCP `topical-authority` tool. Use for content strategy and planning once keyword research is done. To write the articles use sv-prose. ([seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills](https://github.com/seovendorco/sv-mcp-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-topical-authority) · ★2)
- **sv-trend-diagnostic**: Invoke when EVM Schedule Variance (SV) is negative, flat, or worsening across reporting periods and is treated as a status number instead of a corrective trigger, or when a PM must diagnose why a program isn't recovering despite visible schedule pressure. ([StevenACoffman/steve-skill-market](https://github.com/StevenACoffman/steve-skill-market/tree/HEAD/plugins/strategy-leadership/skills/sv-trend-diagnostic))
- **sv-trusteval-c-eval**: Evaluates LLMs' structural and semantic reasoning capabilities in C code vulnerability analysis. It probes whether models rely on memorized patterns or genuinely understand code interdependencies by testing consistency across base, data-flow, control-flow, counterfactual, goal-driven, and predictive scenarios. Use when the user wants to benchmark on SV-TrustEval-C, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Cons_DFL. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/sv-trusteval-c-eval) · ★3)
- **sv-truth-set-comparison**: Use when you have simulated reads with a known embedded SV set (e.g. 900 SVs from SURVIVOR simulateSV) and need to score mapper/tool SV calls (recall, precision, F1) against the truth set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sv-truth-set-comparison))
- **sv-variant-overlap-collapse**: Use when a sample has multiple SV caller VCFs (e.g. the six callers of ConsensuSV-ONT) that must be merged and deduplicated into one overlapping-variant-free candidate VCF before consensus scoring or CNN filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sv-variant-overlap-collapse))
- **sv-vocal-tuning**: 在 Synthesizer V 中按乐句、歌词和演唱风格调教人声音高、咬字、颤音、呼吸及表现参数。适用于人声调教与机械感诊断，不用于音高曲线画图彩蛋。 ([littleclock2/AKDAgent-Plugin](https://github.com/littleclock2/AKDAgent-Plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/akdagent-chatgpt-plugin/skills/sv-vocal-tuning))
- **sv-write**: Entry point for research writing: paper skeletons, Introductions and other sections, polishing and Chinese-to-English rewriting, critique without edits, de-AI editing of papers, reports, slides and letters, and mining or applying venue style. Routes to one sv-write sub-command and says which. Use when the user asks to write, revise, polish, de-AI or style research text. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-write) · ★1)
- **sv-write-critique**: Critiques a draft without editing it: runs the source-recall and verification checklist, the reader-logic checks, the logic-chain consistency checks when a skeleton exists, and the research-core writing checklist, then says whether each problem is language, structure, or a claim the evidence may not carry. Returns findings only. Use when the user asks what is wrong with a draft, wants a critique, or says not to edit. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-write-critique) · ★1)
- **sv-write-humanize-audit**: Runs the report-only de-AI audit: S1 to S7 scores, the five tests (topic-sentence, movability, asymmetry, falsifier, deletion), a gate verdict, and a finding list where every finding carries an edit class for sv-review-loop. Never edits. Use when the user wants a de-AI gate before review or submission, or when sv-review-loop runs its language round. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-write-humanize-audit) · ★1)
- **sv-write-humanize-letter**: Removes machine-written texture from letters and applications: response letters, rebuttals and emails to advisors use the academic-response profile (version narration expected, one polite opener allowed); proposals and grant applications use the academic profile. Same protected-content contract as the paper sub-command. Use when a rebuttal, response letter, email, proposal or grant text reads as AI-generated. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-write-humanize-letter) · ★1)
- **sv-write-humanize-paper**: Removes machine-written texture from a paper, thesis chapter or section under the academic profile: diagnoses restatement, defensiveness, false symmetry and reporting hygiene as S1 to S7 against a claim ledger, then edits only under the contract that protects numbers, citations, terms and LaTeX. Use when a manuscript reads as AI-generated and the user wants it diagnosed or rewritten, or its numbers cleaned up. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-write-humanize-paper) · ★1)
- **sv-write-humanize-report**: Removes machine-written texture from reports: weekly and progress reports, STATUS and other files under docs/ use the docs profile (clarity over voice); technical reports for outside readers use the academic profile. Same diagnosis and editing contract as the paper sub-command, so numbers, citations and terms stay protected. Use when a report, status update or project document reads as AI-generated. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-write-humanize-report) · ★1)
- **sv-write-humanize-slides**: Removes machine-written texture from slide decks: frame titles and bodies use the slides profile (one hedge per claim, a selling point stated once per deck, no slogan closers, title verbs at or below the evidence level), while Beamer note text uses the academic profile. Use when a Beamer or other deck reads as AI-generated, or when the group-meeting deck skill calls for its de-AI audit. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-write-humanize-slides) · ★1)
- **sv-write-intro**: Drafts the Introduction as flowing prose. Technique and new-problem papers follow the six-paragraph flowchart (background and running example, limitations, goal, challenges, solution overview, contributions); RQ-driven empirical studies follow the outsider-question route to research-question blocks. Weaves verified citations; gives an outline only on explicit request. Use when the user asks to draft, outline, or restructure an Introduction. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-write-intro) · ★1)
- **sv-write-polish**: Polishes existing academic prose while preserving the author's meaning: grammar and flow repair, idiomatic wording, section conventions, tone calibration against evidence strength, and fair description of prior work. Light touch by default, one pass only, and every edit that could change scientific meaning is flagged. Use when the user asks to polish a draft, fix awkward wording, smooth a paragraph, or check whether a claim is too strong. - **sv-write-section**: Drafts publishable paper prose from the author's own materials, from one paragraph to a full manuscript, across STEM and non-STEM fields. Builds the claim ledger before prose, traces every factual claim to user input, verified retrieval or field common knowledge, and runs the citation verification ladder. Use when the user asks to write a section, an abstract, turn results or notes into paper text, or draft a full paper. ([easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus](https://github.com/easygl1der/supervisor-skills-plus/tree/HEAD/skills/sv-write-section) · ★1)

Use when the user asks for a paper skeleton, logic chain, thinking template, or a benchmark plan before writing.
